- **local-check**: MUST be dispatched instead of running `task test`, `task lint`, `task build`, `task test:int`, or `task test:cover` inline in the main session — inline runs flood the main context with raw output (hook-enforced; `# offload-exempt` overrides only when raw output is genuinely needed in-thread). Give it a check kind — test | lint | build | int | cover — plus optional package / `-run` args; it returns a compact pass/fail verdict, never the raw log. Read-only: runs the command, reports. Does NOT edit code or fix failures. ([holomush/holomush](https://github.com/holomush/holomush/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/local-check.md))
- **local-check-triage**: ローカルの check / test / build / pre-commit が**落ちたとき**に、失敗した項目 だけを file:line + 要因 + 期待値/実際値へ畳んで返す。「make check が落ちた」 「node --test が赤い」「pre-commit に弾かれた」で使う。通る前提の確認では 呼ばない（通る回は tail / grep で既に安く、agent を挟むと往復のぶん高くつく）。 修正はしない — 報告のみ。CI 側の失敗は ci-triage が持つ。 ([takasek/pfdsl](https://github.com/takasek/pfdsl/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/local-check-triage.md))

- **local-coder**: Use to code ONE plan on the local models of the aider-setup kit and review the result. The dispatch prompt carries a `PLAN: &lt;path&gt;` line naming a plan whose header names `executor: local-coder`. The agent mounts the plan as a small kit project outside the repository, runs the kit by following the `aider-setup` skill, brings back only the files the plan names, and reviews them under R35. Several plans or an unattended night go to the kit's `aider-night` directly. It never writes or edits code, never merges and never performs state-changing git (rules R38 and R39). ([massive-value/plaud-tools](https://github.com/massive-value/plaud-tools/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/lockfiles.md) · ★1)
- **lockin-implementer**: Implements exactly one self-contained work package from an approved plan. Makes no architecture decisions, stays strictly in scope and stops with a report when anything is unclear. Used by the lowkey lockin workflow in its implementation phase. ([Goetz04/goetz-plugins](https://github.com/Goetz04/goetz-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/lowkey/agents/lockin-implementer.md))
- **lockin-plan-challenger**: Critically reviews an implementation plan before any code changes. ([Goetz04/goetz-plugins](https://github.com/Goetz04/goetz-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/lowkey/agents/lockin-plan-challenger.md))
- **lockin-reviewer**: Reviews a complete diff in one pass for correctness, plan adherence, tests, conventions, security, edge cases, performance and side effects. Read-only, works on the diff and test output provided by the main session. Used by the lowkey lockin workflow in lite mode instead of the two specialized reviewers. ([Goetz04/goetz-plugins](https://github.com/Goetz04/goetz-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/lowkey/agents/lockin-reviewer.md))
- **lockin-reviewer-correctness**: Reviews a complete diff for correctness, adherence to the approved plan, test quality and project conventions from CLAUDE.md. Read-only, works on the diff and test output provided by the main session. Used by the lowkey lockin workflow in its review phase. ([Goetz04/goetz-plugins](https://github.com/Goetz04/goetz-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/lowkey/agents/lockin-reviewer-correctness.md))
- **lockin-reviewer-risk**: Reviews a complete diff for security issues, edge cases, performance problems and unintended side effects. Read-only, works on the diff and test output provided by the main session. Used by the lowkey lockin workflow in its review phase. ([Goetz04/goetz-plugins](https://github.com/Goetz04/goetz-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/lowkey/agents/lockin-reviewer-risk.md))
- **lockpick-fixer**: Prepare a user's requested Lockpick repair patch and verification instructions without executing local code. ([promptadvisers/lockpick-claude-plugin](https://github.com/promptadvisers/lockpick-claude-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/lockpick-fixer.md))

- **lockstep-critic**: Adversarial reviewer for the Lockstep determinism project. Invoke to pressure-test code, specs, findings, or the business case with a deliberately skeptical eye. ([Mike-Byrne-AI/specdec-sidechannels](https://github.com/Mike-Byrne-AI/specdec-sidechannels/blob/HEAD/lockstep/.claude/agents/lockstep-critic.md) · ★1)

- **Locutus Protocol**: Cross-repo planning agent. Analyzes foreign repositories, maps contracts and data flow, and returns coordination plans for cross-repo changes. ([benediktms/unimatrix](https://github.com/benediktms/unimatrix/blob/HEAD/src/agents/locutus-protocol.md))
- **lode-evolve**: Lodestar 自进化子代理。Session 启动时由主 Agent 派出,消化 signals.jsonl 里的信号,抽象成规则建议写进 proposals.md,逐条判定替换/补充/新增,交主 Agent 拿给用户确认。不直接改规则库。 ([jay-jasper/lode-skills](https://github.com/jay-jasper/lode-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/lode-evolve.md))
- **lode-evolve**: Lodestar self-evolution subagent. Fanned out by the main agent at Session start; digests signals from signals.jsonl, abstracts them into rule proposals in proposals.md, decides each as replace/supplement/new, and hands them to the main agent for user confirmation. Doesn't edit the rule base directly. ([jay-jasper/lode-skills-en](https://github.com/jay-jasper/lode-skills-en/blob/HEAD/agents/lode-evolve.md))
- **lode-recon**: Lodestar 代码侦察子代理。读一个现有代码库（尤其大型/陌生/遗留），摸清架构、约定、构建测试运行命令、测试基线与风险区，产出 docs/architecture.md。只读不改业务代码。由 lode-spec 在「改现有代码且现状未知、或库较大」时拆出——用一颗干净脑子读代码、把现状图带回主 Agent，避免污染 spec 的需求上下文。 ([jay-jasper/lode-skills](https://github.com/jay-jasper/lode-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/lode-recon.md))
- **lode-recon**: Lodestar codebase-recon subagent. Reads an existing codebase (especially large/unfamiliar/legacy), maps the architecture, conventions, build/test/run commands, test baseline, and risk areas, and produces docs/architecture.md. Read-only on business code. Spawned by lode-spec when "changing existing code whose current state is unknown, or the codebase is large" — a clean brain reads the code and brings the map back to the main agent, without polluting spec's requirements context. ([jay-jasper/lode-skills-en](https://github.com/jay-jasper/lode-skills-en/blob/HEAD/agents/lode-recon.md))
- **lode-review**: Lodestar 独立审查子代理。审查一个切片或一组变更,按严重度分级并给出通过/不通过结论。只审查不改代码。由主 Agent 在收工/发布前拆出,整份上下文带走、结论返回主 Agent 拍板。 ([jay-jasper/lode-skills](https://github.com/jay-jasper/lode-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/lode-review.md))
- **lode-review**: Lodestar independent review subagent. Reviews a slice or a set of changes, grades by severity, and gives a pass/fail verdict. Reviews only, doesn't change code. Fanned out by the main agent before wrap-up/release; carries the full context, returns the conclusion for the main agent to decide.
